knitr::opts_chunk$set(echo = TRUE)
library(mvtnorm)
library(RAS)
library(future.apply)
Loading required package: future
plan(multisession)
start_time <- Sys.time()
source("code/ras_ss.R")
# run config
n_snps    <- 300
n_disc    <- 1000 # discovery cohort, method A (small for speed)
n_targ    <- 1000 # target cohort (small for speed)
rho       <- 0.8 # AR(1) correlation for LD
skip1           <- 10
skip2           <- 3
min_window_size <- 3
max_window_size <- 30
prune_r2        <- 0.2 # LD pruning
causal_snps  <- c(150, 151, 152)
effect_size  <- 0.5
slope_thresh   <- 1e-3
davies_thresh  <- 1e-3
base_seed      <- 35
best_ws        <- 12 # chosen via the window-size sweep in 01_simulation
R_true <- outer(1:n_snps, 1:n_snps, function(i, j) rho^abs(i - j))
set.seed(base_seed)
X_ref <- sim_genotypes(10000, R_true)
R_emp <- cor(X_ref)
prune_filter <- prune_ld(R_emp, prune_r2)
cat("Surviving SNPs after LD pruning:", sum(prune_filter), "of", n_snps, "\n")
Surviving SNPs after LD pruning: 100 of 300 
null_beta   <- rep(0, n_snps)
signal_beta <- rep(0, n_snps)
signal_beta[causal_snps] <- effect_size
# did the validated peak land on the causal cluster (for power calculation)
in_zone <- function(tau) any(tau >= 130 & tau <= 170)
# rows 1 & 2 share the same simulated data per rep (paired test);
# row 3 runs the shipped package pipeline with its own internal 50/50 split.
# row 4 runs the pure ras() function with num_rep = 5 (true published default)
n_null  <- 500
n_power <- 200
n_pure  <- 100 # fewer reps because pure ras() does 5x internal resplits

paired_null <- future_lapply(seq_len(n_null), function(s) suppressWarnings({
  r   <- one_rep(null_beta, seed = 100000 + s)
  ind <- indiv_scan(r$scan, r$X_t, r$y_t, r$b_disc)
  c(ss  = length(detect_peaks(r$scan, best_ws, scw = 8)$val$tau_hats) > 0,
    ind = length(detect_peaks(list(x = r$scan$x, y = ind), best_ws, scw = 8)$val$tau_hats) > 0)
}))

# for signal reps, also save profiles / tau positions for diag
paired_pow <- future_lapply(seq_len(n_power), function(s) suppressWarnings({
  r   <- one_rep(signal_beta, seed = 200000 + s)
  ind <- indiv_scan(r$scan, r$X_t, r$y_t, r$b_disc)
  ss_tau  <- detect_peaks(r$scan, best_ws, scw = 8)$val$tau_hats
  ind_tau <- detect_peaks(list(x = r$scan$x, y = ind), best_ws, scw = 8)$val$tau_hats
  list(ss_zone  = in_zone(ss_tau),
       ind_zone = in_zone(ind_tau),
       ss_tau   = ss_tau,
       ind_tau  = ind_tau,
       scan     = r$scan,
       ind_prof = ind)
}))

pure_p <- sapply(pure_p_tau, in_zone)
# format for table
fmt <- function(k, n) {
  b <- binom.test(sum(k), n)$conf.int
  sprintf("%.3f (%.3f-%.3f)", mean(k), b[1], b[2])
}
knitr::kable(data.frame(
  row   = c("ras-ss", 
           "indiv (same data)", 
           "indiv (original pkg, 50/50 split)", 
           "indiv (pure ras(), num_rep=5)"),
  type1 = c(fmt(ss_n, n_null),  fmt(ind_n, n_null),  fmt(nat_n, n_null),  fmt(pure_n, n_pure)),
  power = c(fmt(ss_p, n_power), fmt(ind_p, n_power), fmt(nat_p, n_power), fmt(pure_p, n_pure)),
  reps  = c(paste0(n_null, " / ", n_power),
            paste0(n_null, " / ", n_power),
            paste0(n_null, " / ", n_power),
            paste0(n_pure, " / ", n_pure))
), caption = "Method comparison -- point estimate (95% CI)")
Method comparison – point estimate (95% CI)
row type1 power reps
ras-ss 0.038 (0.023-0.059) 0.935 (0.891-0.965) 500 / 200
indiv (same data) 0.036 (0.021-0.056) 0.940 (0.898-0.969) 500 / 200
indiv (original pkg, 50/50 split) 0.048 (0.031-0.071) 0.915 (0.867-0.950) 500 / 200
indiv (pure ras(), num_rep=5) 0.010 (0.000-0.054) 1.000 (0.964-1.000) 100 / 100 
# ras-ss uses the same seeds as the published number,
# so this must reproduce 0.038 / 0.935 exactly
cat(sprintf("ras-ss repro check: type1 = %.3f (expect 0.038), power = %.3f (expect 0.935)\n",
            mean(ss_n), mean(ss_p)))

# Profile Comparison
# Plot 4 shows reps where both methods succeeded. 
# If curves overlap, the summary-stat math perfectly matches the individual-level scan.
par(mfrow = c(2, 2), mar = c(4, 4, 2, 1))
success_idx <- which(ss_p & ind_p)[1:4]
for (i in success_idx) {
  plot(paired_pow[[i]]$scan$x, paired_pow[[i]]$scan$y, type = "l", col = "blue", lwd = 2,
       main = paste("Signal Rep", i), xlab = "Pivotal SNP", ylab = "-log10(p)", 
       ylim = c(0, max(paired_pow[[i]]$scan$y, paired_pow[[i]]$ind_prof, na.rm = TRUE) + 1))
  lines(paired_pow[[i]]$scan$x, paired_pow[[i]]$ind_prof, col = "red", lwd = 2)
  abline(v = 151, col = "grey", lty = 2, lwd = 2)
  legend("topright", c("ras-ss", "indiv (paired)"), col = c("blue", "red"), lwd = 2, bty = "n")
}

# do they hit the exact same causal SNP?
# extract all validated tau_hats from the signal runs
ss_taus   <- unlist(lapply(paired_pow, function(x) x$ss_tau))
ind_taus  <- unlist(lapply(paired_pow, function(x) x$ind_tau))
nat_taus  <- unlist(nat_p_tau)
pure_taus <- unlist(pure_p_tau)

par(mfrow = c(1, 1), mar = c(4, 4, 2, 1))
# simple density plot to show clustering around the true causal SNPs (150,151,152)
plot(density(ss_taus, na.rm = TRUE, from = 100, to = 200), col = "blue", lwd = 3, 
     main = "Validated Peak Locations (Signal Reps)", xlab = "SNP Position", xlim = c(100, 200))
lines(density(ind_taus, na.rm = TRUE, from = 100, to = 200), col = "red", lwd = 3)
lines(density(nat_taus, na.rm = TRUE, from = 100, to = 200), col = "darkgreen", lwd = 3)
lines(density(pure_taus, na.rm = TRUE, from = 100, to = 200), col = "purple", lwd = 3)
abline(v = 151, col = "black", lty = 2, lwd = 2)
legend("topright", c("ras-ss", "indiv (paired)", "native 50/50", "pure ras()"), 
       col = c("blue", "red", "darkgreen", "purple"), lwd = 3, bty = "n")
